# Agent LobeHub Fullstack Parity Ledger

> **Status**: active
> **Version**: v0.1
> **Created**: 2026-07-07 | **Updated**: 2026-07-08
> **Owner**: Agent Architecture
> **Module**: `docs/architecture/agent/lobehub-parity/`

---

## 1. Goal

基于 Peers-Touch 当前项目与 `external/lobehub` 源码，按 “BOM -> Spec -> Plan -> Gate -> Evidence -> Traceability” 方法论，完成 Peers-Touch AI Agent 模块对 LobeHub 的全栈能力对标、原型复刻与架构迁移设计。

满分标准：Peers-Touch Agent 在功能覆盖、交互体验、配置能力、Memory、Provider、Tool、Knowledge、Session、Runtime 等核心能力上达到 LobeHub 水平，并适配 Peers-Touch 的 Actor / Station / Desktop 架构。

## 2. Scope Control

### In Scope

- LobeHub Agent/Chat/Provider/Memory/Tool/Knowledge/Session/Runtime 源码级盘点。
- Peers-Touch Agent 当前前后端能力盘点。
- Peers-Touch vs LobeHub 全量能力差距矩阵。
- 高保真 Agent 原型。
- Peers-Touch 全栈迁移架构。
- Actor#agent 社交能力预留边界。
- 后续产品代码迁移计划与 Gate/Evidence 追踪。

### Out of Scope For This Ledger Phase

- 未经原型确认直接迁移产品代码。
- 本轮实现 Actor#agent 社交产品功能。
- 回滚、覆盖、格式化无关 worktree 改动。
- 硬编码默认 Provider/model 列表。

## 4. Spec

| Spec ID | Requirement | Owner | Status | Gate |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| SPEC-001 | Agent 信息架构：Agent 列表、会话列表、Topic、聊天主区、Profile、Settings。 | Agent Architecture | in-progress | GATE-001 |
| SPEC-002 | Chat 主流程：新建、发送、停止、重试、继续生成、重新生成、分支、错误恢复。 | Agent Runtime | in-progress | GATE-001 |
| SPEC-003 | Provider/model：对齐 LobeHub 能力，适配 Peers-Touch Settings Provider；必须支持 provider + model 唯一选择。 | Provider Runtime | in-progress | GATE-005 |
| SPEC-004 | Memory：对齐 LobeHub memory 能力，定义 Peers-Touch Station 侧 source of truth、Desktop 投影和同步边界。 | Station | in-progress | GATE-006 |
| SPEC-005 | Agent Config：系统提示词、角色、人设、开场白、推荐问题、模型、上下文、温度/推理等级等配置。 | Agent Runtime | in-progress | GATE-006 |
| SPEC-006 | Knowledge/File：知识源、文件夹、文档、索引状态、授权状态、错误恢复。 | Station | in-progress | GATE-006 |
| SPEC-007 | Tool/Plugin/Skill：工具启停、授权、风险提示、调用状态、审批、结果展示。 | Agent Runtime | in-progress | GATE-006 |
| SPEC-008 | Session/Topic：会话持久化、自动命名、搜索、删除、重命名、置顶、分组。 | Agent Runtime | in-progress | GATE-006 |
| SPEC-009 | Runtime/Streaming：流式输出、thinking、tool call、error、done、abort、reconcile。 | Agent Runtime | in-progress | GATE-006 |
| SPEC-010 | Frontend Prototype Fidelity：基于 LobeHub 源码高保真复刻，不只看截图。 | Prototype | in-progress | GATE-003/GATE-004 |
| SPEC-011 | Backend Architecture：Station owns cross-end business truth；Desktop owns local UI/runtime projection；Model/proto owns contract semantics。 | Agent Architecture | implemented | GATE-006 |
| SPEC-012 | Actor#agent Reserved Boundary：预留 Actor identity、Agent identity、social graph、capability exposure、permission、event stream，不实现社交产品功能。 | Agent Architecture | implemented | GATE-007 |
| SPEC-013 | Migration Safety：不回滚无关改动，不格式化无关文件，不破坏现有架构。 | All | in-progress | GATE-008 |
| SPEC-014 | License/Attribution：借鉴开源代码时记录来源路径、许可证边界和不可直接复制的资产/文本。 | Agent Architecture | implemented | GATE-001 |

## 9. Decision Log

| ID | Decision | Status | Evidence |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| D-001 | Treat this work as fullstack Agent capability parity, not frontend skinning. | accepted | EVID-000 |
| D-002 | Do not migrate product code before prototype confirmation and fullstack architecture gates. | accepted | EVID-000 |
| D-003 | Settings Provider remains Peers-Touch provider source for Agent model consumption. | accepted | EVID-000 |
| D-004 | Actor#agent social capability is reserved in architecture only for this goal. | accepted | EVID-000 |

## 10. Next Evidence Targets

- EVID-010: HISTORICAL ONLY - REVISION REQUIRED recorded for the rejected prototype.
- EVID-010-R1: HISTORICAL ONLY - revised prototype implementation and browser self-check recorded, but Owner rejected the depth/fidelity as still insufficient.
- EVID-010-R2: HISTORICAL ONLY - live audit and rewrite stream that led to the replacement `agent-lobehub-parity` prototype.
